comes from the Green’s function
\mathbb{G}_{32}
and
\mathbb{G}_{33}
for
\tau\leq\frac{t-1}{2}
, and from
\mathcal{G}(\tau)
when
\tau\geq\frac{t-1}{2}
.

As for
\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}
, similar to (
4.28
), we can represent
\mathbb{G}_{22}
in terms of
H(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}})
and the difference between
\mathbb{G}_{33}
and
H
provides us one more
\delta
. One also observes that all other terms in
\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}
contain quadratic terms except for the first two terms, which contain the linear term
(\theta-1)
. Fortunately, the first two terms only depend on
\mathbb{G}_{32}
, which is much regular than
\mathbb{G}_{33}
. Thus, we can follow similar arguments as in Lemma
4.5
to yield that,

\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}\leq O(1)\left(\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)\frac{1}{\sqrt{1+t}}.

With similar arguments, we follow (
4.35
) to obtain the estimates of the third part
\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
as below,

\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}\leq O(1)\frac{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta}{\sqrt{1+t}}.

Same as before, above estimates hold for
t\geq 1
. When
t\leq 1
, we can follow the arguments in Lemma
4.5
to find similar estimates as
t\geq 1
. Therefore, we combine all above estimates to conclude that,

\displaystyle\|\theta(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}}+O(1)\frac{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}+\frac{\|u(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}^{2}}{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}}+O(1)\frac{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}\right)+\left(\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}}+O(1)\frac{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}\right)^{2}.

\bullet
(
\mathbf{\|\theta(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}}
) For the
L^{1}
estimates of
\theta-1
, we can follow the
L^{1}
estimates of
u
in Lemma
4.5
to obtain the desired results. As the arguments are very similar as the
L^{\infty}
estimates, we omit the details and directly provide the following results,

\displaystyle\|\theta(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)+\frac{1}{2}\|u(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|u(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)+\frac{O(1)\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)+O(1)\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\delta^{2}\right)^{2}.

∎

The first order estimates of
\theta-1
can also be obtained in a similar way. The difference appears only when
\tau
is closed to
t
, i.e., for the remainder
\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
. Actually, we have the following lemma.

Lemma 4.8
.

(\mathbf{\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{1}},\ \|\sqrt{t}\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}})
Let
(v,u,E)
,
C_{\sharp}
,
t_{\sharp}
and
\delta
be the local solution and corresponding parameters constructed in Proposition
1.1
. We further suppose the properties (
4.19
) hold for the solution. Then,
\theta(x,t)
has the following first order estimates at
t\geq t_{\sharp}
,

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{1}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta,\\
&\|\sqrt{t}\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta.\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

According to Lemma
4.2
and Corollary
4.1
,
\theta_{x}
has the following representation,

\displaystyle c_{v}\theta_{x}(x,t)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{31}(x-y,t)(v(y,0)-1)dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{32}(x,t;y,0)u(y,0)dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}G_{33}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\sum_{i=1}^{3}\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{i}-uu_{x},

where the only difference between the representations in Lemma
4.2
and Corollary
4.1
is
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
. As the Green’s function
\mathbb{G}_{3j}
and
\mathbb{G}_{2j}
have similar estimates in Lemma
4.4
, it is obvious that the estimates of the homogeneous terms,
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{1}
and
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}
can be obtained in a similar way as Lemma
4.6
. On the other hand, the way to handle the singularity at
t
in
\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
will be a little bit different from the methods we used for
\mathcal{R}^{u}_{3}
. Thus it is sufficient to provide the details of the estimates of
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
.

\bullet
(
\mathbf{\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{1}}}
) For the
L^{1}
estimates of
\theta_{x}
, we omit the details and provide the estimates of the homogeneous terms,
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{1}
and
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}
as follows,

(4.58)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{21}(x-y,t)(v(y,0)-1)dy\right|dx\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*},\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{22}(x-y,t)u(y,0)dy\right|dx\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*},\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{23}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy\right|dx\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*},\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{1}\right|dx\leq O(1)(1+|\log(\nu_{0})|)\delta^{2},\\
&\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}\right|dx\leq O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta.\end{aligned}\right.

Now, for the third term
\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{3}
, as we only need to consider the case
t\geq t_{\sharp}
and we have already assumed
\mathcal{G}(\tau)<\delta
for
\tau<t
, we directly apply Lemma
4.3
to obtain that the solution can be extended to
t+t_{\sharp}
and has the following estimates,

\|u_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{2C_{\sharp}\delta}{\sqrt{t_{\sharp}}},\quad\|u_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{2C_{\sharp}\delta}{t_{\sharp}},\quad\|\theta_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{2C_{\sharp}\delta}{t_{\sharp}},\quad t_{\sharp}\leq\tau\leq t.

Then, we combine above estimates and the representation (
4.14
) to have the following estimates,

(4.59)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}_{3}^{\theta}\right|dx

\displaystyle\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{xy}\mathbb{G}_{32}(x-y,t-\tau)\left(K(v(y,\tau)-1)+\mu u_{y}\frac{v-1}{v}+\frac{K\theta-Kv}{v}\right)dyd\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\left(\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{33}(x-y,t-\tau)\right)u_{y}(y,\tau)dyd\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\left(1-\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}H\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(u_{y}\left(-p+\frac{\mu u_{y}}{v}\right)+uu_{\tau}\right)dyd\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}H\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}\mu}pu_{y}\right)dyd\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\partial_{x}H\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\frac{\theta_{y}v-\theta v_{y}}{v^{2}}u\right)dyd\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\sum_{z\in\mathscr{D}}\partial_{x}H\left(x,t;z,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}\mu}\frac{K\theta(z,\tau)u(z,\tau)}{v(z,\tau)}\right)\Big|_{z=z^{-}}^{z=z^{+}}d\tau\right|dx

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\left(\delta+\delta^{2}+\delta^{3}\right)+O(1)\left(\delta^{2}+\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t-\nu_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\left(\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\right).

Now, we combine (
4.58
) and (
4.59
) to obtain that,

\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{1}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta.

\bullet
(
\mathbf{\|\theta_{x}(\cdot,t)\|_{L^{\infty}}}
) For the
L^{\infty}
estimates, the above representation is not proper, since the last two terms in (
4.59
) is not integrable with respect to
\tau
if we do not involve the integration of
x
. Therefore, we switch to the representation in Corollary
4.1
to obtain that,

\displaystyle\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}_{3}^{\theta}

\displaystyle=\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{xy}\mathbb{G}_{32}(x-y,t-\tau)\left(K(v(y,\tau)-1)+\mu u_{y}\frac{v-1}{v}+\frac{K\theta-Kv}{v}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\left(\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{33}(x-y,t-\tau)-H_{x}\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\right)u_{y}(y,\tau)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}H_{x}\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(1-\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}\mu}\right)uu_{\tau}dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}H_{x}\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\left(1-\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}\mu}\right)u_{y}\left(K-p+\frac{\mu}{v}u_{y}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{-\infty}^{x}\int_{-\infty}^{y}H_{x}\left(x,t;z,t-\nu_{0};\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)dz\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\frac{(\theta(y,t-\nu_{0})-v(y,t-\nu_{0}))}{v(y,t-\nu_{0})}u(y,t-\nu_{0})dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\frac{(\theta(x,t-\nu_{0})-v(x,t-\nu_{0}))}{v(x,t-\nu_{0})}u(x,t-\nu_{0})

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{-\infty}^{x}\int_{-\infty}^{y}H_{x}\left(x,t;z,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)dz\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\left(\frac{\left(\theta_{\tau}v-\theta v_{\tau}\right)}{v^{2}}u(y,\tau)+\frac{(\theta-v)}{v}u_{\tau}(y,\tau)\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\left(\frac{\left(\theta_{\tau}(x,\tau)v(x,\tau)-\theta(x,\tau)v_{\tau}(x,\tau)\right)}{v^{2}(x,\tau)}u(x,\tau)+\frac{(\theta(x,\tau)-v(x,\tau))}{v(x,\tau)}u_{\tau}(x,\tau)\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt-\int_{x}^{+\infty}\int_{y}^{+\infty}H_{x}\left(x,t;z,t-\nu_{0};\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)dz\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\left(\frac{\theta(y,t-\nu_{0})-v(y,t-\nu_{0})}{v(y,t-\nu_{0})}\right)u(y,t-\nu_{0})dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt-\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{x}^{+\infty}\int_{y}^{+\infty}H_{x}\left(x,t;z,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)dz\left(\frac{\kappa K}{c_{v}\mu}\right)\left(\frac{\left(\theta_{\tau}v-\theta v_{\tau}\right)}{v^{2}}u(y,\tau)+\frac{(\theta-v)}{v}u_{\tau}(y,\tau)\right)dyd\tau.

Similar as in previous
L^{1}
estimates, we apply Lemma
4.3
to obtain the following estimates,

(4.60)

\|u_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{1}}\leq\frac{O(1)\delta}{\sqrt{t_{\sharp}}},\quad\|u_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{O(1)\delta}{t_{\sharp}},\quad\|\theta_{\tau}(\cdot,\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\frac{O(1)\delta}{t_{\sharp}},\quad t_{\sharp}\leq\tau\leq t.

Then, we apply above estimates of time derivatives, the comparison principle Lemma
2.4
in Section
2
, the estimates of the Green’s function
\mathbb{G}
to obtain that,

(4.61)

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}_{3}^{\theta}\right|

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{1+t-\nu_{0}}}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t-\nu_{0}}(\sqrt{\nu_{0}})}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t-\nu_{0}}\sqrt{t-\nu_{0}}}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{(t-\nu_{0})}

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{1+t-\nu_{0}}+O(1)\nu_{0}\left(\frac{\delta^{2}}{\nu_{0}\sqrt{1+t-\nu_{0}}}+\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t-\nu_{0}}\sqrt{1+t-\nu_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\left|H_{x}(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}})-H_{x}\left(x,t;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}v}\right)\right||u_{y}(y,\tau)|dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-\nu_{0}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\int_{\tau}^{t}\left|H_{z}(z,s;y,\tau;\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}})\right|\left|-\frac{K}{c_{v}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{32}(x-z,t-s)\right|dzds|u_{y}(y,\tau)|dyd\tau

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{t}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}|\log(\nu_{0})|\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}}

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\sqrt{t}}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}|\log(\nu_{0})|\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}},

where the last inequality is due to the fact
t\geq t_{\sharp}\geq 4\nu_{0}
. For the homogeneous terms and the other remainder terms, we omit the details and follow previous estimates to obtain that,

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{21}(x-y,t)(v(y,0)-1)dy\right|\leq C(\nu_{0})\frac{\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{t}},

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{22}(x-y,t)u(y,0)dy\right|\leq C(\nu_{0})\frac{\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{t}},

(4.62)

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{23}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy\right|\leq C(\nu_{0})\frac{\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{t}},

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{1}\right|\leq O(1)(1+|\log(\nu_{0})|)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}},

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}\mathcal{R}^{\theta}_{2}\right|\leq O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{t}}.

Now, we combine (
4.61
) and (
4.62
) to obtain the desired estimates, and finish the proof of the lemma.
∎

Finally, we study the estimates of the specific volume
v(x,t)
. We recall the representation of
v(x,t)
in Lemma
4.2
as below,

\displaystyle v(x,t)-1

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{11}(x-y,t)(v(y,0)-1)dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,0)u(y,0)dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dyd\tau.

According to the estimates of the Green’s function
\mathbb{G}
in Theorem
3.1
, Theorem
3.2
and Theorem
3.3
, we immediately know that
\mathbb{G}_{13}
has similar estimates as
\mathbb{G}_{31}
, and
\mathbb{G}_{12}
has similar estimates as
\mathbb{G}_{21}
, which are more regular than the diagonal parts. Then, we have the following lemma.

Lemma 4.9
.

(\mathbf{\|v(\cdot,t)\|_{BV},\ \sqrt{t+1}\|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{\infty}},\ \|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}})
Let
(v,u,E)
,
C_{\sharp}
,
t_{\sharp}
and
\delta
be the local solution and corresponding parameters constructed in Proposition
1.1
. We further suppose the properties (
4.19
) hold for the solution. Then,
v(x,t)
has the following estimates at
t\geq t_{\sharp}
,

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\delta^{2},\\
&\|\sqrt{1+t}(v(\cdot,t)-1)\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\delta^{2},\\
&\|v(\cdot,t)\|_{BV}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}.\end{aligned}\right.

Proof.

First of all, we will apply Theorem
3.1
and Theorem
3.3
and list the estimates of
\mathbb{G}_{11}
,
\mathbb{G}_{12}
and
\mathbb{G}_{13}
for the convenience. For
t\geq 1
, we have the following estimates of
\mathbb{G}_{1j}
,

(4.63)

\displaystyle|\mathbb{G}_{11}(x,t)-e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.64)

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{11}(x,t)-e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.65)

\displaystyle|\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.66)

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)+\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.67)

\displaystyle|\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)+\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t^{\frac{3}{2}}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.68)

\displaystyle|\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.69)

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1,

(4.70)

\displaystyle|\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)-\frac{c_{v}K^{2}}{\kappa\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x)|\leq\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x+\beta_{j}t)^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t^{\frac{3}{2}}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\geq 1.

And for
t\leq 1
, we have the following estimates of
\mathbb{G}_{12}
and
\mathbb{G}_{13}
,

(4.71)

\displaystyle|\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)|\leq O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1,

(4.72)

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)+\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x)|\leq\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{x^{2}}{4Ct}}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1,

(4.73)

\displaystyle|\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t)+\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x)|\leq\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{x^{2}}{4Ct}}}{t}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1,

(4.74)

\displaystyle|\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)|\leq O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1,

(4.75)

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)|\leq O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1,

(4.76)

\displaystyle|\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x,t)-\frac{K}{\kappa\mu}e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x)|\leq\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{x^{2}}{4Ct}}}{\sqrt{t}}+O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}t-\sigma_{0}|x|}+O(1)te^{-\sigma_{0}|x|},\quad t\leq 1.

In the following, we will use two steps to provide the zeroth order and first order estimates of
v(x,t)
respectively.

\bullet
(
\mathbf{\sqrt{1+t}\|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{\infty}},\ \|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}}
)
We will only show the proof of the infinity norm estimates, and the
L^{1}
estimates can be obtained similarly. For the homogeneous terms, as we have
t\geq t_{\sharp}
, we can follow previous arguments to obtain the estimates of
\mathbb{G}_{1j}
for any
t\geq t_{\sharp}
, which are similar as (
4.63
), (
4.65
) and (
4.68
) with coefficients
C(\nu_{0})
. Then, we omit the details and obtain that,

(4.77)

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{11}(x-y,t)(v(y,0)-1)dy\right|\leq\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}},\\
&\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x-y,t)u(y,0)dy\right|\leq\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}},\\
&\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy\right|\leq\frac{C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}}{\sqrt{1+t}}.\end{aligned}\right.

Next, for the inhomogeneous terms, we split it into three parts. This is because the estimates of the Green’s function are different for small and large time, and also the time decay comes from different parts for small time and large time. Actually, we may define for
t\geq 1
that,

(4.78)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{v}_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dyd\tau

(4.79)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{v}_{2}

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dyd\tau

(4.80)

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{v}_{3}

\displaystyle=\int_{t-1}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-1}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dyd\tau.

Similar as before, we only have
\mathcal{R}^{v}_{3}
when
t\leq 1
, and the integration in
\mathcal{R}^{v}_{3}
will be from
0
to
t
. Now, for
\mathcal{R}^{v}_{1}
, the Green’s function
\mathbb{G}_{1j}
will provide the decay, and we apply (
4.66
), (
4.69
) to have for
t\geq 1
that,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{v}_{1}|

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta(x-y)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x-y+\beta_{j}(t-\tau))^{2}}{4C(t-\tau)}}}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}(t-\tau)-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x-y+\beta_{j}(t-\tau))^{2}}{4C(t-\tau)}}}{t-\tau}\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{\frac{t-1}{2}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}(t-\tau)-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dy\right|d\tau

(4.81)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}.

Next, for
\mathcal{R}^{v}_{2}
, the integrability is same as (
4.81
), while the decay now comes from
\mathcal{G}(\tau)
. More precisely, we apply (
4.66
) and (
4.69
) again to have,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{v}_{2}|

\displaystyle=\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta(x-y)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x-y+\beta_{j}(t-\tau))^{2}}{4C(t-\tau)}}}{t-\tau}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}(t-\tau)-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\sum_{j=1}^{3}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x-y+\beta_{j}(t-\tau))^{2}}{4C(t-\tau)}}}{t-\tau}\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\frac{t-1}{2}}^{t-1}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}^{*}(t-\tau)-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dy\right|d\tau

(4.82)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}}.

Next, for
\mathcal{R}^{v}_{3}
, the decay comes from
\mathcal{G}(\tau)
. Moreover, as
\tau
is close to
t
, we have to use the small time estimates of
\mathbb{G}
, and actually we apply (
4.72
) and (
4.75
) to obtain that,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{v}_{3}|

\displaystyle=\int_{t-1}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta(x-y)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-1}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{O(1)e^{-\frac{(x-y)^{2}}{4C(t-\tau)}}}{\sqrt{t-\tau}}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-1}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dy\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{t-1}^{t}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}O(1)e^{-\sigma_{0}|x-y|}\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dy\right|d\tau

(4.83)

\displaystyle\leq O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}}\leq O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{1+t}},

where the last inequality is due to the fact
t\geq 1
. Now if
t\leq 1
, the integration of (
4.83
) becomes from
0
to
t
, and we can simply obtain that similar results. Therefore, we combine (
4.77
), (
4.78
), (
4.79
), (
4.80
), (
4.81
), (
4.82
), (
4.83
) and above discussion for the case
t\leq 1
to obtain that,

\sqrt{1+t}\|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\delta^{2}.

Then, we take one more integration with respect to
x
to obtain the
L^{1}
estimates. We omit the details and conclude that,

\|v(\cdot,t)-1\|_{L^{1}}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\delta^{2}.

\bullet
(
\mathbf{\|v(\cdot,t)\|_{BV}}
) Now, we study the BV norm of the specific volume
v(x,t)
. To this end, we need to further investigate the discontinuity. Actually, we can rewrite the representation of
v(x,t)
as follows,

(4.84)

\displaystyle v(x,t)-1

\displaystyle=e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}(v(x,0)-1)+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{x}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\mathbb{G}_{11}(x-y,t)-e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta(x-y)\right)(v(y,0)-1)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,0)u(y,0)dy+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left(\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)-\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta(x-y)\right)\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{(K\theta-K)(1-v)}{v}+\frac{\mu u_{y}(v-1)}{v}-\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{y}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\left((\frac{K\theta}{v}-K)u+\left(\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right)\theta_{y}+\left(\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right)uu_{y}\right)dyd\tau.

From the representation (
4.84
) and properties of Green’s function, we immediately see that the discontinuity only appears in the first two terms. Therefore, we can estimates the jumps as follows,

(4.85)

\displaystyle\sum_{z\in\mathscr{D}}\left|v(z,t)\Big|_{z=z^{-}}^{z=z^{-}}\right|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\sum_{z\in\mathscr{D}}\left|v(z,0)\Big|_{z=z^{-}}^{z=z^{-}}\right|+O(1)\sum_{z\in\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\left(\|v-1\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\theta-1\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|u_{x}\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\left|v(z,\tau)\Big|_{z=z^{-}}^{z=z^{-}}\right|d\tau

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{*}+O(1)\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\left(\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{1+\tau}}+\frac{\delta}{\sqrt{\tau}}\right)\delta d\tau

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{*}+O(1)\min\left(\delta^{2},\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{t}}\right).

Next, for
x\notin\mathscr{D}
, we can take derivative to (
4.84
) and then integrate in the region
\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}
. More precisely, we apply (
4.67
), (
4.70
), (
4.73
) and (
4.76
) to obtain that,

(4.86)

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\left|v_{x}(x,t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\left|v_{x}(x,0)\right|dx+O(1)\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\left(|v-1||u_{\tau}|+|v-1||\theta_{x}|+|v-1||v_{x}|\right)d\tau dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+O(1)\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\left(|v-1||v_{x}|+|\theta-1||v_{x}|+|\theta_{x}||v-1|+|u||u_{x}|+|u_{x}||v_{x}|\right)d\tau dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{11}(x-y,t)-e^{-\frac{Kt}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x-y)\right||v(y,0)-1|dydx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x-y,t)u(y,0)dy\right|dx+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\partial_{x}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t)\left(E(y,0)-c_{v}\right)dy\right|

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|-\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)-\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x-y)\right|\left(\frac{K(v-1)^{2}}{v}+\frac{|K\theta-K||1-v|}{v}\right)dyd\tau dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|-\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{12}(x,t;y,\tau)-\frac{1}{\mu}e^{-\frac{K(t-\tau)}{\mu}}\delta^{\prime}(x-y)\right|\left(\frac{\mu|u_{y}||v-1|}{v}+\frac{Ku^{2}}{2c_{v}}\right)dyd\tau dx

\displaystyle\hskip 11.38092pt+\int_{\mathbb{R}\setminus\mathscr{D}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left|\partial_{xx}\mathbb{G}_{13}(x-y,t-\tau)\right|\left(\left|\frac{K\theta}{v}-K\right||u|+\left|\kappa-\frac{\kappa}{v}\right||\theta_{y}|+\left|\frac{\kappa}{c_{v}}-\frac{\mu}{v}\right||u||u_{y}|\right)dyd\tau dx

\displaystyle\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}},

where the term
\sqrt{\nu_{0}}
in the last term comes from the estimate of
u_{\tau}
in (
4.60
). Now, we combine (
4.85
) and (
4.86
) to obtain that,

\|v(\cdot,t)\|_{BV}\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{\delta^{2}}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}.

∎

With the a priori estimates in Lemma
4.5
, Lemma
4.6
, Lemma
4.7
, Lemma
4.8
and Lemma
4.9
in hands, we are now ready to prove the global existence of the solution constructed in Proposition
1.1
for sufficiently small initial data.

Theorem 4.1
.

Suppose the initial data
(v_{0},u_{0},\theta_{0})
of Navier-Stokes equation (
1.3
) satisfies

(4.87)

\|v_{0}-1\|_{BV}+\|u_{0}\|_{BV}+\|\theta_{0}-1\|_{BV}+\|v_{0}-1\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{L^{1}}+\|\theta_{0}-1\|_{L^{1}}<\delta^{*}.

for
\delta^{*}
sufficiently small. Then the solution constructed in Proposition
1.1
exists globally in time, and there exists a positive constant
\mathscr{C}
such that,

(4.88)

\|\sqrt{t+1}(v-1)\|_{\infty}+\|\sqrt{t+1}u\|_{\infty}+\|\sqrt{t+1}(\theta-1)\|_{\infty}+\|\sqrt{t}u_{x}\|_{\infty}+\|\sqrt{t}\theta_{x}\|_{\infty}<\mathscr{C}\delta^{*}.

Proof.

We let
C_{\sharp}
,
t_{\sharp}
and
\delta
be the parameters in Proposition
1.1
, then Propositions
1.1
and
1.2
guarantee the existence and uniqueness of weak solution
(v,u,\theta)
in
[0,t_{\sharp})
. Now we let the initial data satisfy the following smallness condition

\|v_{0}-1\|_{BV}+\|u_{0}\|_{BV}+\|\theta_{0}-1\|_{BV}+\|v_{0}-1\|_{L^{1}}+\|u_{0}\|_{L^{1}}+\|\theta_{0}-1\|_{L^{1}}<\delta^{*}.

From Lemma
4.3
, for sufficiently small
\delta^{*}
, we can define a stopping time
T
as in (
4.15
), which has the following properties,

\displaystyle T>t_{\sharp},\qquad\mathcal{G}(T)\geq\delta,\qquad\mathcal{G}(\tau)<\delta\ \mbox{ for all $\tau<T$},

where
\mathcal{G}(\tau)
is defined in (
4.15
). According the the definition of
\mathcal{G}(\tau)
and Lemma
4.3
, we immediately conclude that the life span of the solution
(v,u,\theta)
is larger than
T
. Therefore, in order to prove the global existence of the solution, it suffices to show
T=\infty
for sufficient small
\delta^{*}
. We will prove this by contradiction. Suppose not, then we have

(4.89)

T<+\infty,\quad\mbox{for arbitrary postive $\delta^{*}$ }.

On the other hand, we combine Lemma
4.5
, Lemma
4.6
, Lemma
4.7
, Lemma
4.8
and Lemma
4.9
together to find that, the solution at
T
has the following estimates,

\mathcal{G}(T)\leq C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta+\left(C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}+O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}+O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta\right)^{2},

where
\nu_{0}
is some small positive constant such that
\nu_{0}\leq\frac{t_{\sharp}}{4}
. Actually, it is observed that when we choose
\delta^{*}
and
\delta
to be smaller, all the other parameters such as
C_{\sharp}
and
t_{\sharp}
are uniformly bounded, and thus can be viewed as unchanged. Moreover, all the
O(1)
coefficients in above formula are independent of
\nu_{0}
, and thus the
O(1)
coefficients will be uniformly bounded when
\delta
,
\delta^{*}
and
\nu_{0}
are changed to be smaller. Therefore, we can first choose
\nu_{0}
to be sufficiently small such that

O(1)\sqrt{\nu_{0}}\delta\leq\frac{\delta}{6}.

Then, we fix the
\nu_{0}
, and let
\delta
to be sufficiently small so that

O(1)\frac{|\log(\nu_{0})|}{\sqrt{\nu_{0}}}\delta^{2}\leq\frac{\delta}{6}.

Finally, for fixed small
\nu_{0}
and
\delta
, we let
\delta^{*}
to be sufficiently small, so that we can obtain

(4.90)

C(\nu_{0})\delta^{*}\leq\frac{\delta}{6}.

Now, for these well chosen
\nu_{0}
,
\delta
and
\delta^{*}
, we combine all above estimates to obtain that,

\mathcal{G}(T)\leq\frac{\delta}{2}+\frac{\delta^{2}}{4}\leq\frac{3\delta}{4}<\delta,

which obviously contradicts to the assumption (
4.89
). Thus, the assumption (
4.89
) is false and we conclude that, for sufficiently small positive constant
\delta^{*}
such that (
4.87
) holds, we can find a small positive constant
\delta
such that,

(4.91)

\mathcal{G}(t)<\delta,\quad\mbox{for all $t>0$}.

From previous discussion and the definition of
\mathcal{G}(t)
in (
4.15
), (
4.91
) immediately implies the global existence and uniqueness of the weak solution. Moreover, the large time behavior (
4.88
) directly follows (
4.91
). In view of the relation (
4.90
) between
\delta
and
\delta^{*}
, we finish the proof of the theorem.

∎

(B.3)

\displaystyle M_{3}^{*,0}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&0&0\\
0&0&0\\
0&-u&1\\
\end{array}\right),\quad M_{3}^{*,1}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&0&0\\
0&\frac{uvp_{e}}{\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}}&-\frac{vp_{e}}{\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}}\\
0&-\frac{v\left(p-u^{2}p_{e}\right)}{\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}}&-\frac{uvp_{e}}{\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{3}^{*,2}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&\frac{uv^{2}p_{e}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)}&-\frac{v^{2}p_{e}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)}\\
0&-\frac{pv^{2}p_{e}}{\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&0\\
\frac{pv^{2}p_{v}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)}&-\frac{2puv^{2}p_{e}}{\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&\frac{pv^{2}p_{e}}{\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{3}^{*,3}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&\frac{pv^{3}p_{e}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&0\\
-\frac{pv^{3}p_{e}p_{v}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&\frac{uv^{3}p_{e}\left(p_{v}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-2p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}\right)}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}&\frac{v^{3}p_{e}\left(2p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}+p_{v}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right)\right)}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}\\
-\frac{puv^{3}p_{e}p_{v}}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&\frac{v^{3}\left(p-u^{2}p_{e}\right)\left(2p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}+p_{v}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right)\right)}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}&\frac{uv^{3}p_{e}\left(2p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}+p_{v}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right)\right)}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{3}^{*,4}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}-\frac{pv^{4}p_{e}p_{v}}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{2}}&\frac{uv^{4}p_{e}\left(pp_{e}\left(\mu-3\kappa\theta_{e}\right)+p_{v}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}&\frac{v^{4}p_{e}\left(p_{v}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right)-pp_{e}\left(\mu-3\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}\\
0&\frac{pv^{4}p_{e}\left(2p_{v}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-3p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}\right)}{\kappa\theta_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{4}}&0\\
\frac{pv^{4}p_{v}\left(pp_{e}\left(\mu-3\kappa\theta_{e}\right)+p_{v}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right){}^{3}}&-\frac{puv^{4}p_{e}\left(6p\kappa^{2}p_{e}\theta_{e}^{2}+p_{v}\left(\mu^{2}-6\kappa\theta_{e}\mu+5\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\right)\right)}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{4}}&\frac{pv^{4}p_{e}\left(3p\kappa^{2}p_{e}\theta_{e}^{2}+p_{v}\left(\mu^{2}-4\kappa\theta_{e}\mu+3\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\right)\right)}{\kappa^{2}\theta_{e}^{2}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right){}^{4}}\\
\end{array}\right).

Appendix C
The expression of
M_{j}^{k}
in (
3.54
)

The zeroth order coefficient of the expansion of
\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{j}
are listed below,

\displaystyle M_{1}^{0}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{pp_{e}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}&-\frac{up_{e}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}&\frac{p_{e}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\\
0&0&0\\
-\frac{pp_{v}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}&\frac{up_{v}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}&-\frac{p_{v}}{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{2}^{0}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}-\frac{p_{v}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&\frac{up_{e}-\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&-\frac{p_{e}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}\\
\frac{p_{v}}{2\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}&\frac{1}{2}-\frac{up_{e}}{2\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}&\frac{p_{e}}{2\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}\\
\frac{\left(p+u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)p_{v}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&\frac{-p_{e}\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}u^{2}-p_{v}u+p\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&\frac{p_{e}\left(p+u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{3}^{0}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}-\frac{p_{v}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&\frac{up_{e}+\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&-\frac{p_{e}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}\\
-\frac{p_{v}}{2\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}&\frac{1}{2}\left(\frac{up_{e}}{\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}+1\right)&-\frac{p_{e}}{2\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}\\
\frac{\left(p-u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)p_{v}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&-\frac{-p_{e}\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}u^{2}+p_{v}u+p\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}&\frac{p_{e}\left(p-u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)}{2pp_{e}-2p_{v}}\\
\end{array}\right).

The first order coefficient of the expansion of
\hat{\mathbb{M}}_{j}
are listed below,

\displaystyle M_{1}^{1}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}0&\frac{p\kappa p_{e}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}&0\\
-\frac{p\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}&\frac{u\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}&-\frac{\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
-\frac{pu\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}&-\frac{\kappa\left(p-u^{2}p_{e}\right)p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}&-\frac{u\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{2}^{1}=(\xi_{1},\xi_{2},\xi_{3}),

\displaystyle\xi_{1}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{p_{v}\left(\mu p_{v}-pp_{e}\left(\mu+3\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\frac{p\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{2v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\frac{pp_{v}\left(p_{e}\left(p\mu+\kappa\left(p+2u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}\right)-p_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle\xi_{2}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{p_{e}\left(-2p\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\theta_{e}-up_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)+pup_{e}\left(\mu+\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
-\frac{\left(p_{e}\left(p\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\mu+\left(2u\kappa p_{v}-p\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}\right)-\mu\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}p_{v}\right)}{4v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
-\frac{\left(2p^{2}u\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)p_{e}^{2}+up_{v}\left(\kappa\left(5p+2u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}-3p\mu\right)p_{e}+p_{v}\left(u\mu p_{v}-2p\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle\xi_{3}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}-\frac{p_{e}\left(pp_{e}\left(\mu+\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-p_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\frac{\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{2v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\frac{p_{e}\left(p_{e}\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)p^{2}+p_{v}\left(2\kappa\left(2p+u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}-p\mu\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle M_{3}^{1}=(\zeta_{1},\zeta_{2},\zeta_{3}),

\displaystyle\zeta_{1}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{p_{v}\left(pp_{e}\left(\mu+3\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-\mu p_{v}\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\frac{p\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{2v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\frac{p_{v}\left(pp_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-pp_{e}\left(p\mu+\kappa\left(p-2u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle\zeta_{2}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}-\frac{p_{e}\left(2p\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\theta_{e}-up_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)+pup_{e}\left(\mu+\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\frac{\left(p_{e}\left(p\mu\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}-\kappa\left(\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}p+2up_{v}\right)\theta_{e}\right)-\mu\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}p_{v}\right)}{4v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\frac{\left(2p^{2}u\left(\mu-\kappa\theta_{e}\right)p_{e}^{2}+up_{v}\left(\kappa\left(5p-2u\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\right)\theta_{e}-3p\mu\right)p_{e}+p_{v}\left(u\mu p_{v}+2p\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\end{array}\right),

\displaystyle\zeta_{3}=\left(\begin{array}[]{ccc}\frac{p_{e}\left(pp_{e}\left(\mu+\kappa\theta_{e}\right)-p_{v}\left(\mu-2\kappa\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\frac{\kappa p_{e}p_{v}\theta_{e}}{2v\left(p_{v}-pp_{e}\right){}^{2}}\\
\frac{p_{e}\left(p_{e}\left(\kappa\theta_{e}-\mu\right)p^{2}+p_{v}\left(p\mu+\left(2u\kappa\sqrt{pp_{e}-p_{v}}-4p\kappa\right)\theta_{e}\right)\right)}{4v\left(pp_{e}-p_{v}\right){}^{5/2}}\\
\end{array}\right).
